def remove_node(node):
  for each list type (A, B) and value val:
    pos = position of node in that list (precompute index_in_A[node], index_in_B[node])
    dsu[type][val].parent[pos] = dsu[type][val].find(pos+1)

In iterate_list(type, val, l, r):

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left

sys.setrecursionlimit(1_000_000)

class DSU:
    __slots__ = ("parent",)
    def __init__(self, n):
        self.parent = list(range(n + 1))
    def find(self, x):
        parent = self.parent
        while parent[x] != x:
            parent[x] = parent[parent[x]]
            x = parent[x]
        return x
    def union(self, x):
        # remove index x by linking to x+1
        self.parent[x] = self.find(x + 1)


def solve_case(N, K, A, B):
    tasks = [i for i in range(N) if A[i] != B[i]]
    if not tasks:
        return True

    # positions of tasks only
    A_pos = [[] for _ in range(N + 1)]
    B_pos = [[] for _ in range(N + 1)]
    for i in tasks:
        A_pos[A[i]].append(i)
        B_pos[B[i]].append(i)

    idx_in_A = [-1] * N
    idx_in_B = [-1] * N
    for v in range(1, N + 1):
        for k, p in enumerate(A_pos[v]):
            idx_in_A[p] = k
        for k, p in enumerate(B_pos[v]):
            idx_in_B[p] = k

    dsu_A = [None] * (N + 1)
    dsu_B = [None] * (N + 1)
    for v in range(1, N + 1):
        if A_pos[v]:
            dsu_A[v] = DSU(len(A_pos[v]))
        if B_pos[v]:
            dsu_B[v] = DSU(len(B_pos[v]))

    alive = [False] * N
    for i in tasks:
        alive[i] = True

    def remove_node(x):
        dsu_A[A[x]].union(idx_in_A[x])
        dsu_B[B[x]].union(idx_in_B[x])
        alive[x] = False

    def iter_forward(x):
        # edges x -> y if A[y] == B[x] or B[y] == B[x]
        lst = A_pos[B[x]]
        dsu = dsu_A[B[x]]
        if lst:
            lo = bisect_left(lst, x - K)
            idx = dsu.find(lo)
            L = len(lst)
            while idx < L and lst[idx] <= x + K:
                y = lst[idx]
                if alive[y]:
                    yield y
                    idx = dsu.find(idx)
                else:
                    idx = dsu.find(idx + 1)

        lst = B_pos[B[x]]
        dsu = dsu_B[B[x]]
        if lst:
            lo = bisect_left(lst, x - K)
            idx = dsu.find(lo)
            L = len(lst)
            while idx < L and lst[idx] <= x + K:
                y = lst[idx]
                if y == x:
                    idx = dsu.find(idx + 1)
                    continue
                if alive[y]:
                    yield y
                    idx = dsu.find(idx)
                else:
                    idx = dsu.find(idx + 1)

    # First pass: Kosaraju order
    order = []
    for s in tasks:
        if not alive[s]:
            continue
        stack = [s]
        alive[s] = False
        remove_node(s)
        while stack:
            x = stack.pop()
            order.append(x)
            for y in iter_forward(x):
                if alive[y]:
                    alive[y] = False
                    remove_node(y)
                    stack.append(y)

    # Rebuild for second pass
    A_pos = [[] for _ in range(N + 1)]
    B_pos = [[] for _ in range(N + 1)]
    for i in tasks:
        A_pos[A[i]].append(i)
        B_pos[B[i]].append(i)

    idx_in_A = [-1] * N
    idx_in_B = [-1] * N
    for v in range(1, N + 1):
        for k, p in enumerate(A_pos[v]):
            idx_in_A[p] = k
        for k, p in enumerate(B_pos[v]):
            idx_in_B[p] = k

    dsu_A = [None] * (N + 1)
    dsu_B = [None] * (N + 1)
    for v in range(1, N + 1):
        if A_pos[v]:
            dsu_A[v] = DSU(len(A_pos[v]))
        if B_pos[v]:
            dsu_B[v] = DSU(len(B_pos[v]))

    alive = [False] * N
    for i in tasks:
        alive[i] = True

    def iter_reverse(x):
        # reverse edges: original y -> x
        # condition: B[y] == A[x] or B[y] == B[x]
        lst = B_pos[A[x]]
        dsu = dsu_B[A[x]]
        if lst:
            lo = bisect_left(lst, x - K)
            idx = dsu.find(lo)
            L = len(lst)
            while idx < L and lst[idx] <= x + K:
                y = lst[idx]
                if alive[y]:
                    yield y
                    idx = dsu.find(idx)
                else:
                    idx = dsu.find(idx + 1)

        lst = B_pos[B[x]]
        dsu = dsu_B[B[x]]
        if lst:
            lo = bisect_left(lst, x - K)
            idx = dsu.find(lo)
            L = len(lst)
            while idx < L and lst[idx] <= x + K:
                y = lst[idx]
                if y == x:
                    idx = dsu.find(idx + 1)
                    continue
                if alive[y]:
                    yield y
                    idx = dsu.find(idx)
                else:
                    idx = dsu.find(idx + 1)

    comp = [-1] * N
    comp_has_A = []
    comp_has_B = []
    comp_has_fixed_source = []

    # fixed sources
    fixed_by_value = [[] for _ in range(N + 1)]
    for i in range(N):
        if A[i] == B[i]:
            fixed_by_value[B[i]].append(i)

    comp_id = 0
    for s in reversed(order):
        if not alive[s]:
            continue
        cid = comp_id
        comp_id += 1
        comp_has_A.append(False)
        comp_has_B.append(False)
        comp_has_fixed_source.append(False)

        stack = [s]
        alive[s] = False
        remove_node(s)
        comp[s] = cid
        while stack:
            x = stack.pop()
            for y in iter_reverse(x):
                if alive[y]:
                    alive[y] = False
                    remove_node(y)
                    comp[y] = cid
                    stack.append(y)
                elif comp[y] == cid:
                    # internal edge y -> x
                    if A[x] == B[y]:
                        comp_has_A[cid] = True
                    if B[x] == B[y]:
                        comp_has_B[cid] = True

        # fixed source for this component
        # check tasks in this component
        # We did not store component members; do a quick scan over tasks (acceptable once)
        # To keep linear, we can check during assignment, but simpler:
        # We'll do a separate loop after all comps.

    # Check incident edges and fixed sources
    # For each task, need at least one possible source.
    # Also mark fixed source per component.
    # We'll scan tasks.
    for i in tasks:
        c = comp[i]
        # fixed source?
        lst = fixed_by_value[B[i]]
        if lst:
            lo = bisect_left(lst, i - K)
            if lo < len(lst) and lst[lo] <= i + K:
                comp_has_fixed_source[c] = True

        # incident edge from tasks?
        ok = False
        lst = A_pos[B[i]]
        if lst:
            lo = bisect_left(lst, i - K)
            if lo < len(lst) and lst[lo] <= i + K:
                ok = True
        if not ok:
            lst = B_pos[B[i]]
            if lst:
                lo = bisect_left(lst, i - K)
                idx = lo
                while idx < len(lst) and lst[idx] <= i + K:
                    if lst[idx] != i:
                        ok = True
                        break
                    idx += 1
        if not ok and comp_has_fixed_source[c]:
            ok = True
        if not ok:
            return False

    # For every component, if it has no fixed source,
    # it must contain both A-type and B-type internal edges.
    # This is a necessary condition in the intended characterization.
    for c in range(comp_id):
        if not comp_has_fixed_source[c]:
            if not (comp_has_A[c] and comp_has_B[c]):
                return False

    return True


def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    t = data[0]
    idx = 1
    out = []
    for _ in range(t):
        N = data[idx]; K = data[idx + 1]; idx += 2
        A = data[idx:idx + N]; idx += N
        B = data[idx:idx + N]; idx += N
        out.append("Yes" if solve_case(N, K, A, B) else "No")
    print("\n".join(out))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
